<p>This page lists all API deprecations, along with recommended replacements.</p>

stacks:group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:layers-1-accent-layer stacks:group-active/btn:bg-fill-button-ghost-hover stacks:group-has-[:active]/btn:bg-fill-button-ghost-hover style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>Anthropic uses the following terms to describe the model lifecycle:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Active:</strong> The model is fully supported and recommended for use.</li>
<li><strong>Legacy:</strong> The model will no longer receive updates and might be deprecated in the future.</li>
<li><strong>Deprecated:</strong> The model is still functional but no longer recommended. Anthropic provides a recommended replacement and assigns a retirement date.</li>
<li><strong>Retired:</strong> The model is no longer available for use. Requests to retired models fail.</li>
</ul>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-prose-box="" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal break-words bg-warning text-warning shadow-ring-warning [--cds-prose-code-color:currentColor] [--cds-prose-marker-color:currentColor] [--cds-text-link-color:var(--cds-text-warning)] stacks:bg-page stacks:layers-1-warning stacks:shadow-none prose-block print:break-inside-avoid print:[:is(h2,h3,h4)+&amp;]:break-after-avoid"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p>Deprecated models are likely to be less reliable than active models. Move workloads to active models to maintain the highest level of support and reliability.</p></div></aside>
<p>The dates on this page apply to Anthropic-operated platforms: the Claude API, <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-platform-on-aws">Claude Platform on AWS</a>, <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-models-on-google-cloud/anthropic-managed">Claude Models on Google Cloud (Anthropic managed)</a>, and <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-in-microsoft-foundry">Microsoft Foundry</a>. Partner-operated platforms, Amazon Bedrock and Claude Models on Google Cloud (Google managed), set their own retirement schedules, so a model’s lifecycle status and dates can differ. See the <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-in-amazon-bedrock#supported-models">Amazon Bedrock</a>, <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-on-amazon-bedrock-legacy#api-model-ids">Amazon Bedrock (Opus 4.6 and earlier)</a>, and <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-models-on-google-cloud/google-managed#api-model-ids">Claude Models on Google Cloud (Google managed)</a> model tables.</p>

<p>Once a model is deprecated, migrate all usage to a suitable replacement before the retirement date. Requests to models past the retirement date will fail.</p>
<p>To help measure the performance of replacement models on your tasks, consider thorough testing of your applications with the new models well before the retirement date.</p>
<p>For specific instructions on migrating to the latest Claude models, see <a href="/docs/en/about-claude/models/migration-guide">Migration guides</a>.</p>

<p>To help identify usage of deprecated models, you can access an audit of your API usage. Follow these steps:</p>
<ol>
<li>Go to the <a href="/usage">Usage</a> page in Claude Console.</li>
<li>Click <strong>Export</strong>.</li>
<li>Review the downloaded CSV to see usage broken down by API key and model.</li>
</ol>
<p>This audit helps you locate any instances where your application is still using deprecated models, allowing you to prioritize updates to newer models before the retirement date.</p>

<ol>
<li>Regularly check the documentation for updates on model deprecations.</li>
<li>Test your applications with newer models well before the retirement date of your current model.</li>
<li>Update your code to use the recommended replacement model as soon as possible.</li>
<li>Contact the support team if you need help with migration or have any questions.</li>
</ol>
<h2 id="deprecation-downsides-and-mitigations" class="group">Deprecation downsides and mitigations<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><span role="status" aria-live="polite" class="sr-only"></span><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 stacks:[&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-100 stacks:[&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:text-button-disabled stacks:[&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:[--cds-shortcut-cap-ink:currentColor] stacks:[&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:not-in-data-[cds=SplitDropdownButton]:[--cds-text-button-disabled:var(--cds-fizzy-lake-color-icon-only-disabled)] absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100 data-[copied]:opacity-100 touch:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to Deprecation downsides and mitigations" id="_R_a4vmd5bsnqknp5upfllpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish stacks:transform-none!"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] stacks:transition-[box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent  stacks:group-hover/btn:bg-fill-button-ghost-hover stacks:group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-button-ghost-hover stacks:group-hover/btn:group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:layers-1-fill-button-layer stacks:group-[:disabled:not([aria-busy])]/btn:bg-transparent stacks:group-[:disabled:not([aria-busy])]/btn:group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-transparent stacks:group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:layers-1-accent-layer stacks:group-active/btn:bg-fill-button-ghost-hover stacks:group-has-[:active]/btn:bg-fill-button-ghost-hover stacks:group-active/btn:layers-1-fill-button-layer stacks:group-has-[:active]/btn:layers-1-fill-button-layer "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>Anthropic currently deprecates and retires models to ensure capacity for new model releases. This comes with downsides:</p>
<ul>
<li>Users who value specific models must migrate to new versions.</li>
<li>Researchers lose access to models for ongoing and comparative studies.</li>
<li>Model retirement introduces safety- and model welfare-related risks.</li>
</ul>
<p>At some point, Anthropic hopes to make past models publicly available again. In the meantime, Anthropic has committed to long-term preservation of model weights and other measures to help mitigate these impacts. For more details, see <a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/deprecation-commitments" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commitments on Model Deprecation and Preservation</a>.</p>

<p>Anthropic occasionally deprecates request parameters that no longer apply to current models. A new model can reject a deprecated parameter, and a new <a href="/docs/en/api/versioning">API version</a> can remove it for every model.</p>
<div class="prose-scroll relative print:overflow-visible"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>Status</th><th>Behavior</th><th>Recommended replacement</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class=""><code>temperature</code>, <code>top_p</code>, <code>top_k</code></td><td class="">Removed in <a href="/docs/en/api/migration-2026-10-21#sampling-parameters">API version <code>2026-10-21</code></a></td><td class="">Returns a 400 error. Earlier API versions return it when set to a non-default value on Claude 4.7 and later models and <a href="https://anthropic.com/glasswing" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Claude Mythos Preview</a>.</td><td class="">Omit and use <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/prompt-engineering/claude-prompting-best-practices">prompting</a> to guide model behavior.</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>For migration steps, see <a href="/docs/en/about-claude/models/migration-guide">Migration guides</a>.</p></div><div class="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-sm border-t pt-6 print:hidden"><p
